> 119	### PROC-CAD-02: Tick + roteamento por `plan_kind` (FR-CAD-004 / SDD §3.2 topo)
> 120	
> 121	```
> 122	START (pg_cron */15, GMT — o tick VIVO autopilot-cadence-cron; NENHUM job novo)
> 123	  │
> 124	  ▼
> 125	[Edge Fn: autopilot-cadence-cron]
> 126	  ├── Gate: Authorization === Bearer SB_SECRET_KEY ──(fail)──▶ HTTP 403 "Forbidden"
> 127	  ├── SELECT plans WHERE next_run_at <= now() AND is_active   (índice parcial due — reusado)
> 128	  │        (user_id SERVER-TRUSTED da LINHA, nunca do body — SOP autopilot-cron-identity.md)
> 129	  │
> 130	  ├──▶ <Gateway: plan_kind?>
> 131	  │        │ 'viral'                          │ 'cadence'
> 132	  │   [dispatch → autopilot-run]        [dispatch → cadence-run]   (PROC-CAD-03)
> 133	  │   (fluxo existente, intocado)             │
> 134	  │                                     fan-out BOUNDED (MAX_PER_RUN=50 / CONCURRENCY=6)   (FM-CAD-07)
> 135	  │
> 136	  └── pg_cron não sobrepõe a MESMA job (fila) ⇒ tick anterior ainda rodando não empilha
> 137	       ▼
> 138	END
> 139	```
> 140	
> 141	**Gates de verificação:** `SELECT count(*) FROM cron.job` **inalterado** antes/depois (NFR-CAD-012 — 0 job novo); POST ao tick sem `Bearer SB_SECRET_KEY` → **403**; log mostra `cadence-run` para linhas `plan_kind='cadence'` e `autopilot-run` para `'viral'`.
> 142	
> 143	> **Recovery:** tick que falha em N linhas registra `infra_health_logs` por linha e **continua** o batch (fan-out isolado). pg_cron re-dispara em 15 min; `next_run_at` não avança para linhas não processadas ⇒ recuperação natural na próxima janela (limitada por `catchup_window`).
> 144	
> 145	---
> 146	
> 147	### PROC-CAD-03: Ciclo de cadência — `cadence-run` (FR-CAD-006, o coração / SDD §3.2)
> 148	
> 149	```
> 150	START (invocado por PROC-CAD-02 · Auth Bearer SB_SECRET_KEY → 403; user_id da LINHA)
> 151	  │
> 152	  ▼
> 153	[1) DRAIN: SELECT ... FROM autopilot_plans WHERE due AND plan_kind='cadence' FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED]   (evita contenção multi-consumer)
> 154	  │
> 155	  ├──▶ <Gateway: overlap_policy=skip — ciclo anterior deste plano ainda rodando?>
> 156	  │        │ SIM ──▶ [SKIP: não empilha] ──▶ re-arm janela seguinte
> 157	  │        │ NÃO
> 158	  ▼
> 159	[2) RESOLVE SUBJECT owner-scoped: creative_assets.id (Fatia 1 = asset existente ⇒ 0 mco, keyless)]   (OTD-CAD-002=A)
> 160	  │        └─(asset não pertence ao user_id da linha)──▶ HALT + infra_health_logs
> 161	  ▼
> 162	[3) GATE CHAIN — server-side, NUNCA LLM]:
> 163	  ├── quiet_hours(fuso do sujeito, cascata tz→profiles.timezone→America/Sao_Paulo) ──(dentro)──▶ HALT (suprime, não reenfileira)   (FR-CAD-008)
> 164	  ├── frequency_cap(user_id, channel) vs teto channel_profiles.cadence ──(estourou)──▶ HALT + toast pt-BR (Knock: HALT, não reenfileira)   (FR-CAD-008 · OTD-CAD-008=autoritativo · OTD-CAD-017=(user,channel))
> 165	  ├── opt-out: withdrawn_at checado NO SEND, independente da base legal ──(revogado)──▶ HALT + cascata   (G3/G8 · FM-CAD-04)
> 166	  ├── jurisdição: lead sem país ⇒ tratar como UE (opt-in prévio, fail-closed)   (G11 · OTD-CAD-015=consent fail-closed BR)
> 167	  ├── dedup/digest: digestKey=(user,channel,dia) colapsa N vencimentos em 1 publicação   (FR-CAD-009)
> 168	  └── A/B: mod(abs(hashtext(subject_ref||':'||experiment_key)::bigint),100) < ratio   (FR-CAD-010 — cast bigint ANTES do abs; NUNCA %100 nem random())
> 169	  │
> 170	  ▼
> 171	[4) DISPATCH: INSERT scheduled_posts (metadata.reshape + schedule:true + publish_at ISO)]   (Amendment 22 — o nó ENFILEIRA, não publica)
> 172	  │        └─(data inválida)──▶ 422 · (double-enqueue)──▶ 409 GROUP MARKER
> 173	  ▼
> 174	[5) LEDGER: INSERT cadence_dispatches ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING RETURNING]
> 175	  │        (índice único PARCIAL WHERE status<>'failed' — falha LIBERA a chave / sucesso PRENDE)   (FR-CAD-003 · FM-CAD-01)
> 176	  │        └─(conflito ⇒ 0 linhas)──▶ [já despachado neste occurrence: NO-OP anti double-post]
> 177	  ▼
> 178	[6) RE-ARM: next_run_at ← próximo horário em UTC (calculador {frequency,days,hours,minutes,tz})  |  deactivate se fim de programa]   (FR-CAD-007)
> 179	  │
> 180	  ▼
> 181	[Insert mcorch_node (observação) no 1º ciclo bem-sucedido do plano]   (Mesh Connection Mandate · Pattern P8)
> 182	  │
> 183	  ▼
> 184	END
> 185	        ▼ (assíncrono, desacoplado)
> 186	[PROC-CAD-04 auto-publish DRENA scheduled_posts → publica]
> 187	        ▼
> 188	[PROC-CAD-05 reconcile webhook de STATUS → cadence_dispatches.status='sent' + external_usd_cost + cost_source]   (NUNCA do 202 — FM-CAD-05, Lei 1)
> 189	```
> 190	
> 191	**Swimlanes:**
> 192	
> 193	| Swimlane | Participant | Tasks |
> 194	|----------|-------------|-------|
> 195	| Cron/Edge | `autopilot-cadence-cron` → `cadence-run` | Drain `SKIP LOCKED`; gate chain; dispatch; ledger; re-arm |
> 196	| DB | `autopilot_plans` · `cadence_dispatches` · `scheduled_posts` · `channel_profiles.cadence` | Índice parcial de idempotência; teto de frequência; sink |
> 197	| Ledger financeiro | `begin`/`finalize` (só se houver passo `generate`) | Débito atômico advisory-lock; refund idempotente; G7 = invariante do ciclo |
> 198	| Mesh | `mcorch_nodes` · `infra_health_logs` | Nó de observação por ciclo; telemetria em todo path de falha |
> 199	
> 200	**Gates de verificação (materiais):** 1 plano vencido → **1** linha `cadence_dispatches` + **1** linha `pending` em `scheduled_posts`; tick sobreposto/retry → **0** 2º dispatch para o mesmo `(plan_id, step_index, occurrence_at)` (smoke re-executável); **nenhuma** linha `sent` a partir de 202; `next_run_at` avança em UTC.
> 201	
> 202	> **Recovery paths:**
> 203	> - **Falha no dispatch (passo 4):** ledger recebe `status='failed'` ⇒ o índice parcial **libera** a `idempotency_key` ⇒ o próximo tick **retenta** o mesmo `occurrence_at` (at-least-once — OTD-CAD-006=A). Backoff por contagem (molde `auto-publish:200-212`).
> 204	> - **Backlog storm** (queda de N horas): `catchup_window` (6h) **limita** quantos vencimentos atrasados disparam; `jitter_seconds` espalha; fan-out bounded (FM-CAD-07).
> 205	> - **Kill-switch:** `is_active=false` no plano (por limiar conservador auto-imposto de feedback negativo — FM-CAD-14) **para** o drain daquele plano no próximo tick.
> 206	
> 207	---
> 208	
> 209	### PROC-CAD-04: Drain + publicação — `auto-publish` (FR-CAD-018 / OTD-CAD-004)
> 210	
> 211	```
> 212	START (pg_cron */5 — MIGRADO de crontab de SO; Fatia 0 item 5)
> 213	  │
> 214	  ▼
> 215	[SELECT ... FROM scheduled_posts WHERE status='pending' AND scheduled_at<=now() FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED LIMIT <batch>]
> 216	  │        (dois runs concorrentes NUNCA reivindicam a mesma linha)   (FM-CAD-01)
> 217	  ▼
> 218	[Resolve asset owner-scoped NA HORA (metadata.reshape) → publica via seam existente (reshape-pillar / publish-space-asset 409 / publish-space-carousel)]
> 219	  ├──(sucesso)──▶ [UPDATE status='published']
> 220	  │                     ▼
> 221	  │              (a plataforma emitirá webhook de STATUS → PROC-CAD-05)
> 222	  └──(erro)──▶ <Gateway: retry_count ≤ N?>
> 223	                   │ SIM ──▶ [retry_count+1; backoff por contagem] ──▶ próxima janela
> 224	                   │ NÃO  ──▶ [UPDATE status='failed'] + infra_health_logs
> 225	                                   ▼
> 226	                                END
> 227	```
> 228	
> 229	**Gates de verificação:** `SELECT jobname FROM cron.job WHERE command ILIKE '%auto-publish%'` ≥1 linha; claim contém `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`; smoke de concorrência = **0** double-publish.
> 230	
> 231	> **Nota de fronteira:** `auto-publish` é **reusado sem tocar na lógica de publicação** — a Fatia 0 só troca o **supervisor** (crontab de SO → pg_cron) e adiciona `SKIP LOCKED`. A cadência **não reconstrói distribuição** (mandato de integração).
> 232	
> 233	---
> 234	
> 235	### PROC-CAD-05: Reconciliação por webhook de status (NFR-CAD-002 / FM-CAD-05)
> 236	
> 237	```
> 238	START (webhook de STATUS da plataforma — NUNCA o 202 de aceite da API)
> 239	  │
> 240	  ▼
> 241	[Verificar identidade: HMAC timingSafeEqual (webhook)  |  ES256 JWKS (se JWT)]  ──(fail)──▶ 401
> 242	  │
> 243	  ▼
> 244	[Casar evento ↔ cadence_dispatches (target_ref / external id)]
> 245	  │
> 246	  ├──(status=delivered/sent)──▶ [UPDATE cadence_dispatches SET status='sent', external_usd_cost=<custo>, cost_source=<fonte BYOK>]   (service-role)
> 247	  │                                    ▼
> 248	  │                             [Agregar em collective_efficiency_ledger]  (custo BYOK MEDIDO mesmo com cobrança mco=0 — NFR-CAD-008)
> 249	  │
> 250	  └──(status=failed/rejected)──▶ [UPDATE status='failed' (libera idempotency_key p/ retry) + infra_health_logs]
> 251	       ▼
> 252	     END
> 253	```
> 254	
> 255	**Gates de verificação:** **nenhuma** linha transiciona para `sent` sem um webhook de status casado (auditoria de reconcile 100%); `external_usd_cost` numérico gravado **no reconcile** (não no 202); cobrança em mco = **0** para canais BYOK cujo custo o tenant absorve, com o custo ainda **registrado** (Lei 1 — "não cobrar ≠ não medir").
> 256	
> 257	> **Recovery:** webhook de status ausente por T > SLA do provider ⇒ reconciliação por **poll** (molde do poll de `video_renders`) reconcilia o órfão; nunca declarar `sent` por timeout (isso seria o falso-sucesso do 202).
> 258	
> 259	---
> 260	
> 261	### PROC-CAD-06: Eliminação LGPD em cascata — `erase_lead()` (FR-CAD-017 / G9 / OTD-CAD-006)
> 262	
> 263	```
> 264	[Trigger: rota pública de opt-out por token assinado  |  pedido de eliminação (LGPD Art. 16/18 VI)]
> 265	  │
> 266	  ▼
> 267	[SELECT erase_lead('<lead_uuid>')]  (SECURITY DEFINER, search_path='', tenant-guarded)
> 268	  │  ── tudo NA MESMA TRANSAÇÃO:
> 269	  ├── UPDATE scheduled_posts SET status='cancelled' WHERE lead_id=<lead> AND status='pending'
> 270	  ├── UPDATE cadence_dispatches SET status='failed', error='erased' WHERE bound-ao-lead AND status IN ('queued','publishing')
> 271	  ├── anonimiza/apaga leads + lead_events + marketing_consents (comportamento existente)
> 272	  │
> 273	  ├──(qualquer passo falha)──▶ ROLLBACK atômico (nada apagado pela metade)
> 274	  ▼
> 275	END
> 276	```
> 277	
> 278	**Gates de verificação:** pós-`erase_lead`, `count(*) scheduled_posts pending`=**0** e `count(*) cadence_dispatches IN(queued,publishing)`=**0** para o lead; atomicidade comprovada (rollback se qualquer passo falhar). Sem esta cascata o direito de eliminação é **ficção** (P0 de conformidade).
> 279	
> 280	---
> 281	
> 282	### PROC-CAD-07 `[PROBE-GATED]`: Laço inbound IG (FR-CAD-012/013 / SDD §3.3)
> 283	
> 284	> ⚠️ **FLUXO CONGELADO.** Nada abaixo da linha `instagram-webhook` gera código antes do **probe FM-CAD-02** fechar: DM real de **terceiro sem role no app** aparecendo no banco + **payload cru** de `entry.messaging` arquivado (FM-CAD-13). Se não chegar sob Standard Access ⇒ **App Review é pré-requisito** e a Fatia 2 inteira está bloqueada.
> 285	
> 286	```
> 287	[scheduled_posts → auto-publish publica no IG (PROC-CAD-04)]
> 288	  │  (o post provoca comentário/DM)
> 289	  ▼
> 290	[instagram-webhook]  (HMAC timingSafeEqual + hub.challenge — esqueleto REUSADO; app secret GLOBAL, OTD-CAD-018)
> 291	  ├──▶ <Gateway: shape do payload?>
> 292	  │        │ entry.changes: comments        │ entry.messaging: DM  ⚠️ shape PROVADO por payload cru real (FM-CAD-13)
> 293	  ▼
> 294	[INSERT lead_events(event_type='message_received') + leads + notify()]  (lead_events entra HOJE, sem migration)
> 295	  ▼
> 296	[UPSERT social_threads / social_messages]  (Meta só devolve as 20 últimas ⇒ histórico mora AQUI; csw_expires_at 24h + private_reply_deadline 7d derivados no SERVIDOR)
> 297	  ▼
> 298	[GATE CHAIN de resposta — server-side, NUNCA LLM]:
> 299	  ├── FK NOT NULL p/ inbound: fila referencia lead_events(message_received) do MESMO user_id ⇒ cold DM IMPOSSÍVEL de representar   (G1 · FM-CAD-03)
> 300	  ├── classificador in/out-window ──(fora da janela)──▶ nada promocional (só template APPROVED ou draft HITL)   (G6)
> 301	  ├── Human Agent tag INALCANÇÁVEL do caminho service-role/pg_cron (separação de caminho, não if)   (G5)
> 302	  ├── caps por-APP + kill-switch GLOBAL + telemetria de feedback negativo por-app   (OTD-CAD-018)
> 303	  │
> 304	  ├──▶ <Gateway: origem?>
> 305	  │        │ comentário                              │ DM in-window
> 306	  │   [private reply 1× comment→DM]            [resposta free-form ≤24h CSW]
> 307	  │   UNIQUE(comment_id) · deadline 7d ·            │
> 308	  │   idade checada NO SEND (2º reply = 23505)       │
> 309	  ▼
> 310	[Nasce delivery:'gated' (molde nurture-dispatch:150) — flip p/ 'sent' só com GO Sovereign]
> 311	  ▼
> 312	[Resposta com link afiliado + UTM → creative_metrics → collective_efficiency_ledger]
> 313	  ▼
> 314	END
> 315	```
> 316	
> 317	**Gates de verificação (materiais):** o **probe precede o código** — `lead_events(message_received)` de DM real de terceiro sem role aparece por `SELECT` (UUID) + payload cru arquivado; enfileirar DM **sem** inbound → **viola FK**; 2º private reply p/ o mesmo `comment_id` → **23505** (`UNIQUE`); dispatch fora da janela de 24h → **bloqueado no send**; a Human Agent tag não é setável por nenhum caminho service-role; todo dispatch nasce `delivery:'gated'`.
> 318	
> 319	> **Recovery:** janela de 24h (CSW) ou 7d (private reply) expirada ⇒ o gate de janela **no send** descarta o dispatch (nunca enfileira para o futuro — cold DM = ban do app, não erro HTTP). Feedback negativo acima do limiar conservador ⇒ **kill-switch global** (`is_active=false` por-app).
> 320	
> 321	---
> 322	
> 323	### PROC-CAD-08: Canais desimpedidos BYOK — Telegram / Email Resend (FR-CAD-014)
> 324	
> 325	```
> 326	[Passo do program resolve canal BYOK: 'telegram' | 'email']
> 327	  │
> 328	  ├── Telegram ──▶ [resolveProviderKey(admin,userId,'telegram',keyId)]
> 329	  │        ├──(sem token)──▶ HTTP 402 { error:'telegram_not_configured', action:'Configure em /dashboard/settings' }
> 330	  │        ├── opt-in estrutural: chat que nunca deu /start ──▶ BLOQUEADO
> 331	  │        └──(ok)──▶ envia (US$0, Meta-free)
> 332	  │
> 333	  └── Email (Resend) ──▶ [resend_api_key per-user cifrada + domínio verificado do tenant]
> 334	           ├──(sem key / domínio não verificado)──▶ 402 estruturado / flip barrado
> 335	           ├── monta rodapé (endereço postal + identificação de anúncio + opt-out) + disclosure de IA SERVER-SIDE, concatenado ao corpo   (G10/G12 · FM-CAD-12 — NUNCA no prompt do LLM)
> 336	           └── flip nurture-dispatch delivery:'gated' → 'sent' SÓ com GO Sovereign + domínio verificado
> 337	```
> 338	
> 339	**Gates de verificação:** Telegram sem token → **402** `telegram_not_configured` com `action`; envio a chat sem `/start` bloqueado; corpo do e-mail contém rodapé/opt-out/disclosure **concatenado server-side** (prova por diff do payload, ausente no prompt); flip `gated→sent` só com flag de GO + domínio verificado (`SELECT` do estado).
> 340	
> 341	---
> 342	
> 343	## 3. Recurrence-Engine Blueprint (substitui a seção n8n — MCORCH é Postgres-first)
> 344	
> 345	> Este módulo **não** usa n8n. O motor de recorrência é **pg_cron + `next_run_at` + índice parcial due**, o padrão que o MCORCH já roda 2× em prod e que a Cadência **estende** (OTD-CAD-003=A). Nenhum job novo (NFR-CAD-012).
> 346	
> 347	**Driver:** `autopilot-cadence-cron` (VIVO, `*/15` GMT)
> 348	**Idempotency key:** `hash(plan_id, step_index, occurrence_at)` sob `UNIQUE INDEX ... WHERE status<>'failed'`
> 349	
> 350	```
> 351	[Trigger: pg_cron */15 → autopilot-cadence-cron]  (gate Bearer SB_SECRET_KEY → 403)
> 352	  │
> 353	  ▼
> 354	[SELECT plans due (índice parcial next_run_at<=now() AND is_active)]  — user_id server-trusted da LINHA
> 355	  │
> 356	  ├── plan_kind='viral'  ──▶ [HTTP → autopilot-run]   (intocado)
> 357	  │
> 358	  └── plan_kind='cadence'──▶ [HTTP → cadence-run]      (PROC-CAD-03)
> 359	           ├── (success) ──▶ [cadence_dispatches ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING RETURNING → re-arm next_run_at UTC]
> 360	           │                        │
> 361	           │                        ▼
> 362	           │                 [scheduled_posts enfileirado → auto-publish drena (desacoplado)]
> 363	           │
> 364	           └── (error) ──▶ [status='failed' (libera idempotency_key) + retry_count+1]
> 365	                    │
> 366	              (count ≤ N) ──▶ [backoff por contagem] ──▶ próximo tick retenta o mesmo occurrence_at
> 367	                    │
> 368	              (count > N) ──▶ [Insert infra_health_logs {service:'spaces-cadence', event:'cadence_dispatch_failed'}]
> 369	                                    │
> 370	                              [notify() Sovereign]
> 371	```
> 372	
> 373	**Data Mapping (inspector → plano → dispatch):**
> 374	
> 375	| Input Field | Source | → | Output Field | Destination |
> 376	|-------------|--------|---|--------------|-------------|
> 377	| `recurrence {frequency,days,hours,minutes,tz}` | `CadencePlanRequest` | → | `next_run_at` (UTC) | `autopilot_plans` |
> 378	| `channel_allowlist` (⊆ auto-publish MENOS X) | inspector | → | `channel` por dispatch | `cadence_dispatches` |
> 379	| `budget_cap_mco` (NOT NULL) | inspector | → | teto de ocorrências | gate chain (PROC-CAD-03) |
> 380	| `sourceAssetId` (owner-scoped) | inspector | → | `metadata.reshape` asset | `scheduled_posts` |
> 381	| `SB_SECRET_KEY` | env (infra) | → | `Authorization` | header cron-only |
> 382	
> 383	> **Idempotência (regra dura):** re-rodar o tick sobre o mesmo dado **produz o mesmo resultado** — o `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` sob o índice parcial garante 0 double-post (falha libera / sucesso prende — Trigger.dev/River). É o **predicado `WHERE status<>'failed'`** (e só ele) que impede FM-CAD-01/03.
> 384	
> 385	---
> 386	
> 387	## 4. Orchestration Integration Points
> 388	
> 389	> A Cadência **não** injeta passo no `orchestrate-content`. Ela **consome** o output a montante (asset/pilar já produzido) e **enfileira** no sink `scheduled_posts`. A tabela abaixo mapeia os **seams de integração vivos** que os fluxos acima tocam — sem reconstruir nenhum.
> 390	
> 391	| Trilho vivo | Peça | Papel no fluxo | mcoCoins | FR |
> 392	|-------------|------|----------------|----------|----|
> 393	| Autopilot | `autopilot_plans` (ESTENDIDA) + `autopilot-cadence-cron` | Plano recorrente + tick roteador (`plan_kind`) | 0 no tick | FR-CAD-002/004 |
> 394	| `scheduled_posts` (sink) | contrato `metadata.reshape` + `schedule:true` + `publish_at` (Amendment 22) | PROC-CAD-03 **enfileira**; PROC-CAD-04 **drena** | 0 no enqueue | FR-CAD-006/018 |
> 395	| sequences/`nurture-dispatch` | modelo `steps jsonb` + `current_step` + `delivery:'gated'` | Molde do `program jsonb`; canal email nasce gated | por dispatch (BYOK) | FR-CAD-014 |
> 396	| `lead_events`/`leads`/`marketing_consents` | ledger append-only + consent gate + `erase_lead()` | Inbound (PROC-CAD-07) + eliminação (PROC-CAD-06) | — | FR-CAD-012/017 |
> 397	| Ledger financeiro | `begin`/`finalize` atômico + `collective_efficiency_ledger` | Cobrança só se houver passo `generate`; custo BYOK medido no reconcile | atômico via RPC | NFR-CAD-007/008 |
> 398	
> 399	**Integration code stub (roteamento no tick existente — extensão in-place, NÃO função nova):**
> 400	```typescript
> 401	// autopilot-cadence-cron: roteia por plan_kind (FR-CAD-004) — nenhum job pg_cron novo
> 402	for (const plan of duePlans) {                 // user_id server-trusted da LINHA
> 403	  const target = plan.plan_kind === 'cadence' ? 'cadence-run' : 'autopilot-run'
> 404	  await invokeEdge(target, { plan_id: plan.id }, `Bearer ${SB_SECRET_KEY}`)
> 405	  // fan-out BOUNDED: MAX_PER_RUN=50 / CONCURRENCY=6 (FM-CAD-07)
> 406	}
> 407	```
> 408	
> 409	---
> 410	
> 411	## 5. Error Handling & Retry Strategy
> 412	
> 413	| Process | Step | Error Type | Max Retries | Backoff | Failure Action |
> 414	|---------|------|-----------|-------------|---------|----------------|
> 415	| PROC-CAD-01 | `cadence-plan` JWT | ES256 inválido/ausente | 0 | — | HTTP 401 imediato |
> 416	| PROC-CAD-01 | `cadence-plan` validação | `budget_cap_mco` ausente · `minutes%5≠0` · canal fora da allowlist | 0 | — | HTTP 422 {field, msg pt-BR} |
> 417	| PROC-CAD-02 | tick auth | sem `Bearer SB_SECRET_KEY` | 0 | — | HTTP 403 |
> 418	| PROC-CAD-03 | dispatch → `scheduled_posts` | data inválida · double-enqueue | 0 | — | 422 · 409 GROUP MARKER (anti-duplicata) |
> 419	| PROC-CAD-03 | ledger `cadence_dispatches` | conflito de idempotência | — | — | `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` (0 linhas ⇒ NO-OP, anti double-post) |
> 420	| PROC-CAD-03 | ciclo (transitório) | provider 5xx / timeout | N (backoff) | Exponencial (molde `auto-publish:200-212`) | `status='failed'` **libera** chave ⇒ retry no próximo tick (at-least-once) |
> 421	| PROC-CAD-03 | backlog storm | queda de N horas | limitado por `catchup_window` | `jitter_seconds` espalha | fan-out bounded (FM-CAD-07) |
> 422	| PROC-CAD-04 | `auto-publish` publish | plataforma 5xx | ≤ N (retry_count) | por contagem | `status='failed'` + `infra_health_logs` |
> 423	| PROC-CAD-05 | reconcile | webhook de status ausente | — | poll (molde `video_renders`) | reconcilia órfão; **nunca** `sent` por timeout (FM-CAD-05) |
> 424	| PROC-CAD-07 | send DM/reply | janela expirada (24h/7d) | 0 | — | descarta no send (cold DM = ban; nunca enfileira p/ futuro) |
> 425	| PROC-CAD-08 | Telegram/Resend | credencial BYOK ausente | 0 | — | HTTP 402 estruturado `{error, action}` fail-closed |
> 426	| Any | falha não-tratada | 5xx interno | — | — | `infra_health_logs {service:'spaces-cadence'}` + `notify()` |
> 427	
> 428	> **Regra de reconciliação (Lei 1):** nenhuma linha vira `sent` sem webhook de status casado. Retry **libera** a `idempotency_key` (predicado `WHERE status<>'failed'`); sucesso **prende**. Kill-switch (`is_active=false`) para o drain do plano por limiar conservador de feedback negativo (FM-CAD-14).
> 429	
> 430	---
> 431	
> 432	## 6. SLA Definitions
> 433	
> 434	| Process | Step | Expected Duration | Timeout Threshold | Alert if exceeded |
> 435	|---------|------|------------------|-------------------|-------------------|
> 436	| PROC-CAD-01 | `cadence-plan` (arm) | < 800 ms p95 | 5 s | Sim → `infra_health_logs` |
> 437	| PROC-CAD-02 | tick + roteamento | < 30 s | 60 s | Sim (job overrun; pg_cron não sobrepõe) |
> 438	| PROC-CAD-03 | `cadence-run` por batch | < 5 s / batch de 50 (NFR-CAD-013) | 30 s | Sim → `infra_health_logs` |
> 439	| PROC-CAD-04 | `auto-publish` drain | < 5 min (ciclo `*/5`) | 10 min | Sim |

> //   server (supabase.auth.getUser with an anon-key client bound to the caller's Authorization header — the
> //   SAME living pattern as canvas-execute:184-211 / autopilot-run:78-83). getUser() validates the JWT
> //   signature server-side (it is NOT a blind atob of the token). user_id is server-trusted from the verified
> //   claim, NEVER from the request body. Cron callers (Bearer SB_SECRET_KEY) are rejected 403 — this endpoint
> //   is arm-only (the drain/dispatch live in cadence-run, invoked by the tick).
> //
> // MANDATE OF INTEGRATION: extends the LIVING rail autopilot_plans (no parallel table, no new pg_cron job).
> //   The live autopilot-cadence-cron tick routes due plans by plan_kind → cadence-run (FR-CAD-004).
> 
> import { createClient } from "https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2";
> 
> const corsHeaders = {

>   "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "POST, OPTIONS",
>   "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type",
> };
> 
> const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}$/;
> const DEFAULT_TZ = "America/Sao_Paulo";        // final fallback of the fuso cascade (FR-CAD-007)
> const FREQUENCIES = new Set(["daily", "weekly", "monthly"]);
> // Fatia 1 (OTD-CAD-011): X/Twitter is OUT of the cadence allowlist until the pay-per-use probe closes
> // (Post-with-link = 45 mco, a Sovereign-gated Console action). Reject it at the edge with a 422.
> const X_CHANNELS = new Set(["x", "twitter"]);

> // ── UTC re-arm calculator (FR-CAD-007 / FM-CAD-06) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> // The cron runs in GMT and NEVER carries a time-of-day preference: the subject's wall-clock time in its IANA
> // timezone is converted to a UTC instant and stored in next_run_at. Deno has no tz library, so DST-correct
> // conversion is done with Intl.DateTimeFormat offsets (the well-known zoned-time → UTC algorithm).
> 
> // Offset (ms) such that local = utc + offset, at a given instant, for a timezone.
> function tzOffsetMs(instant: Date, tz: string): number {
>   const dtf = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
>     timeZone: tz, hourCycle: "h23",
>     year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit",
>     hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", second: "2-digit",
>   });
>   const p: Record<string, string> = {};
>   for (const { type, value } of dtf.formatToParts(instant)) p[type] = value;
>   const asUTC = Date.UTC(+p.year, +p.month - 1, +p.day, +p.hour, +p.minute, +p.second);
>   return asUTC - instant.getTime();
> }
> 
> // A wall-clock (y,mo,d,h,mi) in `tz` → the UTC instant it denotes (refined once across DST boundaries).
> function zonedWallToUtc(y: number, mo: number, d: number, h: number, mi: number, tz: string): Date {
>   const wallAsUtc = Date.UTC(y, mo, d, h, mi, 0);
>   const off = tzOffsetMs(new Date(wallAsUtc), tz);
>   let ts = wallAsUtc - off;
>   const off2 = tzOffsetMs(new Date(ts), tz);
>   if (off2 !== off) ts = wallAsUtc - off2;   // DST fold/gap refinement
>   return new Date(ts);
> }
> 
> // The caller's local calendar date "today" in `tz`.
> function localYmd(instant: Date, tz: string): { y: number; mo: number; d: number } {
>   const dtf = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
>     timeZone: tz, year: "numeric", month: "2-digit", day: "2-digit",
>   });
>   const p: Record<string, string> = {};
>   for (const { type, value } of dtf.formatToParts(instant)) p[type] = value;
>   return { y: +p.year, mo: +p.month - 1, d: +p.day };
> }
> 
> // ISO weekday 1=Mon..7=Sun for a calendar date (tz-independent given Y-M-D).
> function isoDow(y: number, mo: number, d: number): number {
>   const js = new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo, d)).getUTCDay();   // 0=Sun..6=Sat
>   return js === 0 ? 7 : js;
> }
> 
> interface Recurrence {
>   frequency: "daily" | "weekly" | "monthly";
>   days: number[];        // weekly: ISO weekdays 1=Mon..7=Sun · monthly: days-of-month 1..31 · daily: ignored
>   hours: number;         // 0..23
>   minutes: number;       // 0..59, multiple of 5 (Knock semantics)
>   tz: string;            // IANA
> }
> 
> // Next occurrence strictly after `from`, as a UTC Date. Scans ≤ 430 calendar days (covers monthly day-31).
> function computeNextRunAt(rec: Recurrence, from: Date): Date | null {
>   const { y, mo, d } = localYmd(from, rec.tz);
>   const base = Date.UTC(y, mo, d);   // local "today" midnight as a pure-calendar anchor
>   for (let i = 0; i <= 430; i++) {
>     const cand = new Date(base + i * 86_400_000);
>     const cy = cand.getUTCFullYear(), cmo = cand.getUTCMonth(), cd = cand.getUTCDate();
>     let match = false;
>     if (rec.frequency === "daily") match = true;
>     else if (rec.frequency === "weekly") match = rec.days.includes(isoDow(cy, cmo, cd));
>     else if (rec.frequency === "monthly") match = rec.days.includes(cd);
>     if (!match) continue;
>     const utc = zonedWallToUtc(cy, cmo, cd, rec.hours, rec.minutes, rec.tz);
>     if (utc.getTime() > from.getTime()) return utc;
>   }
>   return null;
> }
> 
> function isValidTz(tz: unknown): tz is string {
>   if (typeof tz !== "string" || !tz) return false;
>   try { new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { timeZone: tz }); return true; } catch { return false; }
> }
>

> 2	import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
> 3	import { edgeErrorMessage } from "@/lib/edge";
> 4	
> 5	/**
> 6	 * Poll de uma geração Veo 3.1 (Amendment 25, FR-SPACES-090).
> 7	 *
> 8	 * O Veo é long-running SEM webhook: o `canvas-execute` devolve 202 + `execution_id` com o débito já
> 9	 * feito (ledger-first), e este hook chama `veo-poll` até `done`/`error`. O seam server-side é quem
> 10	 * baixa o MP4 (o URI do Veo exige a chave no header — ela NUNCA chega ao browser), materializa no
> 11	 * bucket privado e finaliza o ledger (estorno integral em falha).
> 12	 *
> 13	 * Molde: `useVoiceRenderPoll` (useAvatarVoice.ts:83) — intervalo fixo, para nos estados terminais.
> 14	 */
> 15	
> 16	export type VeoPollStatus = "running" | "done" | "error";
> 17	
> 18	export interface VeoPollResult {
> 19	  status: VeoPollStatus;
> 20	  execution_id: string;
> 21	  video_url?: string | null;
> 22	  asset_id?: string | null;
> 23	  bytes?: number;
> 24	  refunded?: number;
> 25	  message?: string;
> 26	  error?: string;
> 27	  transient?: boolean;
> 28	}
> 29	
> 30	const TERMINAL: ReadonlySet<VeoPollStatus> = new Set<VeoPollStatus>(["done", "error"]);
> 31	
> 32	export function useVeoPoll(generationId: string | undefined) {
> 33	  return useQuery({
> 34	    queryKey: ["veo-poll", generationId],
> 35	    enabled: Boolean(generationId),
> 36	    // O Veo leva 1–3 min; 6s é o meio-termo entre latência percebida e chamadas desperdiçadas.
> 37	    refetchInterval: (query) => {
> 38	      const status = (query.state.data as VeoPollResult | undefined)?.status;
> 39	      return status && TERMINAL.has(status) ? false : 6000;
> 40	    },
> 41	    queryFn: async (): Promise<VeoPollResult> => {
> 42	      const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke("veo-poll", {
> 43	        body: { generation_id: generationId },
> 44	      });
> 45	      if (error) throw new Error(await edgeErrorMessage(error, "Falha ao consultar o vídeo."));
> 46	      return data as VeoPollResult;
> 47	    },
> 48	  });
> 49	}
> 50

I'm checking the per-channel MCO cost source of truth against the FRD example showing "X-post-com-link (45 mco)". The edge function returns the projected cost from the client since the inspector handles the projection, but there's no declared per-channel cost SSOT in the BoK documentation—it's marked as a TODO in the edge layer, which means the inspector needs to calculate the cost per channel multiplied by occurrences up to the cap.

For Fatia 1's allowed channels (Instagram, TikTok, YouTube, Facebook, LinkedIn, Pinterest, WordPress), publishing is free at 0 MCO since the publishSocial sink has an estimated cost of 0. The FRD still requires displaying the projection formula even though the actual projected cost for these channels is 0, and the budget cap acts as the ceiling. Since the sealed BoK doesn't provide a per-channel MCO cost table, I need to define a local channel cost map with a TODO reference to the FRD. So the projection is calculated as perCycleCost multiplied by the number of occurrences within the budget cap—if the cap is 1350 and each cycle costs 45, that gives 30 occurrences and a total projection of 1350. The display should show this projected total along with the per-cycle cost and occurrence count.

For channels in Fatia-1 that cost 0 mco, I'll mark them as free in that tier while still respecting the cap, which keeps the information transparent.

Since the per-channel mco values aren't formally declared in the data model, I'm leaving a TODO and defaulting to 0 for free Fatia-1 sinks and 45 for X (as documented in the FRD, though X is currently blocked). Now I need to figure out which channels to include in the allowlist for the inspector—the FRD specifies it should be a subset of the auto-publish destinations minus X, so I'm checking what channels are available in the publish destinations.

> -- Existing plan_kind='viral' plans stay valid (mode/interval_days/daily_cap/is_active/next_run_at intact).
> -- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 
> ALTER TABLE public.autopilot_plans
>   -- allowlist stops being a hardcoded subset CHECK; it derives from channel_profiles/channel_allowlist.
>   DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS autopilot_plans_platforms_check,
>   -- the tick routes by this column (FR-CAD-004): viral->autopilot-run | cadence->cadence-run.
>   ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS plan_kind text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'viral'
>       CHECK (plan_kind IN ('viral','cadence')),
>   -- {frequency:'daily'|'weekly'|'monthly', days:int[], hours:int, minutes:int (multiple of 5), tz:IANA};
>   -- next_run_at is computed in UTC on the server (FR-CAD-007 / FM-CAD-06).
>   ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS recurrence        jsonb,
>   -- {start, end, days:int[]} in the SUBJECT's timezone (FR-CAD-008 / gate G3).
>   ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS quiet_hours       jsonb,
>   -- previous cycle still running => skip, never stack (FM-CAD-01).
>   ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS overlap_policy    text DEFAULT 'skip',
>   -- caps the backlog after downtime (FM-CAD-07).
>   ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS catchup_window    interval DEFAULT '6 hours',
>   -- anti-thundering-herd on the fan-out (FM-CAD-07).
>   ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS jitter_seconds    int DEFAULT 0,
>   -- steps publish|wait|throttle|digest|branch — mold of sequences.steps + current_step (P1/P6), not a new format.
>   ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS program           jsonb,
>   -- channels PER PLAN (subset of auto-publish allowlist MINUS X in Fatia 1 — OTD-CAD-011); not a constant.
>   ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS channel_allowlist text[],
>   -- budget cap in mcoCoins (never USD); cadence requires > 0, enforced at the edge with 422 (FR-CAD-011).
>   -- PRE-EXISTS from viral-autopilot (NOT NULL CHECK >= 12) — IF NOT EXISTS reuses it unchanged (see note above).
>   ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS budget_cap_mco    int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;
> 
> COMMENT ON COLUMN public.autopilot_plans.plan_kind IS
>   'spaces-cadence FR-CAD-002/004: viral (legacy) | cadence. The live autopilot-cadence-cron tick routes by this.';
> COMMENT ON COLUMN public.autopilot_plans.recurrence IS
>   'spaces-cadence FR-CAD-007: {frequency,days,hours,minutes(mult of 5),tz(IANA)}; next_run_at computed in UTC.';
> COMMENT ON COLUMN public.autopilot_plans.channel_allowlist IS
>   'spaces-cadence OTD-CAD-011: per-plan channel subset (auto-publish allowlist MINUS X in Fatia 1).';
> 
> -- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> -- PART 2 — cadence_dispatches (NOVO — FR-CAD-003 / FM-CAD-01)
> -- Per-attempt dispatch ledger. NOT append-only pure (OTD-CAD-006): status transitions => service-role UPDATE.
> -- The partial unique index is what ACTUALLY prevents double-post (failure RELEASES the key / success LOCKS it).
> -- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 
> CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.cadence_dispatches (
>   id                uuid        PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
>   user_id           uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,  -- tenant isolation; RLS = auth.uid()
>   plan_id           uuid        NOT NULL,                    -- origin plan (composite FK below is the tenant guard)
>   step_index        int         NOT NULL,                    -- program step (idempotency-key component)
>   occurrence_at     timestamptz NOT NULL,                    -- due instant (idempotency-key component)
>   idempotency_key   text        NOT NULL,                    -- stable hash of (plan_id, step_index, occurrence_at)
>   channel           text        NOT NULL,                    -- dispatch channel (in the plan's channel_allowlist)
>   status            text        NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queued',   -- queued|publishing|sent|failed (TRANSITIONS)
>   target_ref        text,                                    -- scheduled_posts.id / external id (1:1 with the sink)
>   external_usd_cost numeric,                                 -- BYOK external cost, written ON RECONCILE, never on the 202 (FM-CAD-05)
>   cost_source       text,                                    -- cost origin ('meta_ai_provider_br','x_post_url', ...)
>   error             text,                                    -- failure reason ('erased' on the LGPD cascade)
>   created_at        timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
>   updated_at        timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
>   -- TENANT-SCOPED COMPOSITE FK (data-model §3.2): forbids a dispatch pointing at another tenant's plan even
>   -- if plan_id is guessed. Anchors on autopilot_plans UNIQUE(id,user_id) [constraint autopilot_plans_id_user_uniq,
>   -- already live] — same pattern as autopilot_cycles/creative_metrics/campaign_steps.
>   CONSTRAINT cadence_dispatches_plan_fk
>     FOREIGN KEY (plan_id, user_id)
>     REFERENCES public.autopilot_plans (id, user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
> );
> 
> -- Idempotency (FM-CAD-01/03): partial UNIQUE — failure RELEASES the key (retry), success LOCKS it (anti double-post).
> -- Paired with `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING RETURNING` in cadence-run.
> CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS cadence_dispatches_idem
>   ON public.cadence_dispatches (idempotency_key)
>   WHERE status <> 'failed';
> 
> -- Drain-by-plan index (data-model §3.2): scan a plan's due/in-flight dispatches.
> CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS cadence_dispatches_plan_status_idx
>   ON public.cadence_dispatches (plan_id, status);
> 
> -- RLS: default-deny. Client READS own rows; NEVER writes (status/cost transition only via service-role reconcile).
> ALTER TABLE public.cadence_dispatches ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
> 
> -- SELECT own only. No INSERT/UPDATE policy for authenticated => denied by default-deny; the client can never
> -- forge status='sent' nor external_usd_cost. service_role bypasses RLS and writes status/error/cost on reconcile.
> CREATE POLICY "cadence_dispatches_select_own"
>   ON public.cadence_dispatches
>   FOR SELECT
>   USING (user_id = auth.uid());
> 
> -- updated_at audit on every status transition (reuses the shared trigger fn).
> CREATE TRIGGER set_cadence_dispatches_updated_at
>   BEFORE UPDATE ON public.cadence_dispatches
>   FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.update_updated_at_column();
> 
> COMMENT ON TABLE public.cadence_dispatches IS
>   'spaces-cadence FR-CAD-003: per-attempt dispatch ledger. NOT append-only (OTD-CAD-006) — status transitions '
>   '(queued|publishing|sent|failed) via service-role. Partial UNIQUE(idempotency_key) WHERE status<>''failed'' is '
>   'what prevents double-post (FM-CAD-01). Tenant-scoped composite FK (plan_id,user_id)->autopilot_plans(id,user_id). '
>   'RLS select_own; INSERT/UPDATE service-role only. external_usd_cost/cost_source written on reconcile, never the 202.';
> === grep cadence_dispatches anywhere ===
> supabase/functions/cadence-run/index.ts
> supabase/migrations/20260716120000_cadence_recurrence.sql
> ```

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

The migration file creates the `cadence_dispatches` table and modifies `autopilot_plans`, but this table isn't showing up in the auto-generated types file, which suggests either the migration hasn't been applied to the database used for type generation or the types haven't been regenerated since. Since I can't manually edit the types file, I need to figure out which of these is the actual issue.

For the poll hook, I primarily need `next_run_at` from `autopilot_plans` (which is in the types and type-safe) and a list of recent dispatches. I can query `autopilot_plans` directly without issues, but querying `cadence_dispatches` will cause a TypeScript error since the table name isn't recognized in the Database type. The standard workaround is to cast the supabase client to `any` when querying tables that haven't made it into the types yet — that's the escape hatch used elsewhere in the codebase when types lag behind migrations. I'll make the poll defensive by querying `next_run_at` from `autopilot_plans` (which is typed) and wrapping the recent dispatches query in a try/catch that returns an empty array on error—this keeps the UI resilient regardless of whether the migration and types are fully synced in the environment. I just need to filter by id and user_id, both of which are already typed, so that's straightforward.

The RLS policy on `autopilot_plans` correctly scopes clients to their own plans, and the edge function returns `plan_id`, `next_run_at`, and `projected_cost_mco`—the inspector computes the cost locally and sends it back so the ledger stays consistent. Now I'm thinking through the per-channel cost map, since Fatia-1 channels publish for free. Defining the channel cost mapping... cap" means floor(cap / perCycleCost), so I'll stick with that straightforward calculation. For the inspector UI, I need to let users pick a creative asset (image or video) scoped to the owner, set up recurrence controls like frequency toggles and time selection with timezone support, optionally configure quiet hours, select allowed channels, and set a budget cap with a minimum threshold.

The database enforces budget_cap_mco >= 12, so I'll default to 100 and set the client-side minimum to 12 with a helper note explaining the constraint. Now I'm ready to start building the actual files—I'll begin with the type definitions, making sure to place the CadenceData interface in the right spot and verify the makeNodeId import is available.

Adding "cadence" to the CanvasNodeKind union...

</details>

```json
  "content": "import { memo } from \"react\";\nimport { Handle, Position, type NodeProps } from \"@xyflow/react\";\nimport { CalendarClock, CheckCircle2, Loader2, Repeat } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\";\nimport { type CadenceData } from \"@/types/canvas\";\n\nconst HANDLE_CLASS = \"!h-3 !w-3 !rounded-full !border-2 !border-border !bg-card\";\n\nconst FREQ_LABEL: Record<CadenceData[\"recurrence\"][\"frequency\"], string> = {\n  daily: \"di\u00e1ria\",\n  weekly: \"semanal\",\n  monthly: \"mensal\",\n};\n\n// spaces-cadence Fatia 1 (FR-CAD-001) \u2014 Cadence Publish node: input-only sink (arms a recurring plan; no output).\n// The G7 quote==charge invariant belongs to the CYCLE, not this node \u2014 arming is free (0 mco).\nexport const CadenceNode = memo(function CadenceNode({ data, selected }: NodeProps) {\n  const d = data as unknown as CadenceData;\n  const armed = d.status === \"success\" && Boolean(d.planId);\n  const nextRun = d.output?.nextRunAt;\n  return (\n    <div\n      className={cn(\n        \"w-[220px] overflow-hidden rounded-lg border bg-card text-foreground shadow-sm transition-all\",\n        selected ? \"border-accent ring-2 ring-accent/30\" : \"border-border\",\n      )}\n    >\n      {/* Sink: consumes an optional upstream image/video as the cadence subject; no source handle. */}\n      <Handle type=\"target\" position={Position.Left} id=\"input_asset\" className={HANDLE_CLASS} />\n      <header className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 border-b border-border px-2.5 py-1.5\">\n        <CalendarClock className=\"h-3 w-3 text-secondary\" />\n        <span className=\"truncate text-[11px] font-medium\">{d.label}</span>\n        <span className=\"ml-auto inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 font-mono text-[9px] uppercase text-muted-foreground\">\n          <Repeat className=\"h-2.5 w-2.5\" /> {FREQ_LABEL[d.recurrence.frequency]}\n        </span>\n      </header>\n      <div className=\"space-y-1 px-2.5 py-2\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px]\">\n          <span className=\"truncate\">\n            {d.channelAllowlist.length > 0 ? (\n              <span className=\"text-foreground\">{d.channelAllowlist.length} canal{d.channelAllowlist.length > 1 ? \"is\" : \"\"}</span>\n            ) : (\n              <span className=\"text-muted-foreground\">Escolha os canais</span>\n            )}\n            <span className=\"text-muted-foreground\"> \u00b7 teto {d.budgetCapMco} mco</span>\n          </span>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-mono text-[9px] text-muted-foreground\">\n          {d.status === \"running\" ? (\n            <><Loader2 className=\"h-2.5 w-2.5 animate-spin\" /> armando\u2026</>\n          ) : armed ? (\n            <>\n              <CheckCircle2 className=\"h-2.5 w-2.5 text-emerald-400\" />\n              {nextRun ? `pr\u00f3xima: ${new Date(nextRun).toLocaleString(\"pt-BR\", { day: \"2-digit\", month: \"2-digit\", hour: \"2-digit\", minute: \"2-digit\" })}` : \"plano armado\"}\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            <>recorr\u00eancia {FREQ_LABEL[d.recurrence.frequency]} \u00b7 gr\u00e1tis</>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n});\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/src/hooks/useCadencePlan.ts",
  "content": "import { useMutation, useQuery } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\nimport { supabase } from \"@/integrations/supabase/client\";\nimport { edgeErrorMessage } from \"@/lib/edge\";\nimport type { CadenceRecurrence, CadenceQuietHours, CadenceStep } from \"@/types/canvas\";\n\n/**\n * spaces-cadence Fatia 1 \u2014 client hook for the Cad\u00eancia node (FR-CAD-005 arm + poll).\n *\n * ARM (`useCadencePlan`): a TanStack Query mutation that POSTs to the `cadence-plan` edge fn (verify_jwt=false \u2192\n *   the fn verifies the USER JWT server-side; identity is server-trusted, user_id never from the body). The fn\n *   upserts autopilot_plans (plan_kind='cadence') and ARMS next_run_at in UTC from `recurrence` (FR-CAD-007).\n *   Creating/re-arming a plan costs 0 mco (SDD \u00a74 \u2014 the G7 quote==charge invariant is a per-CYCLE begin/finalize\n *   invariant, NOT this node). Pass `planId` to re-arm an existing owner-scoped cadence plan.\n *\n * POLL (`useCadenceRunPoll`): reads `autopilot_plans.next_run_at` + the plan's recent `cadence_dispatches`\n *   (both SELECT-own RLS) on a fixed interval \u2014 the mold of `useVeoPoll` (id lives in the node `data`, so it\n *   survives a browser refresh; never `sleep(8s)\u00d725`). Cadence is recurrent (no terminal state), so the poll\n *   simply refreshes while a plan is armed. Fail-soft: if the dispatches ledger is not yet reflected in the\n *   generated Supabase types for this environment, that leg returns [] and next_run_at still renders.\n *\n * SSOT: docs/bok/spaces-cadence/{04-frd,05-sdd,06-data-model,07-process-flow}.md. Conven\u00e7\u00f5es: l\u00f3gica/vars em\n * ingl\u00eas, mensagens de UI em pt-BR; nenhuma coluna/RPC inventada al\u00e9m da su\u00edte selada.\n */\n\nexport interface CadencePlanRequest {\n  planId?: string;                 // present \u21d2 re-arm an existing cadence plan (owner-scoped UPDATE)\n  sourceAssetId: string;           // creative_assets.id (owner-scoped subject) \u2014 0 mco keyless (OTD-CAD-002)\n  recurrence: CadenceRecurrence;   // computed in UTC on the server (FR-CAD-007)\n  quiet_hours?: CadenceQuietHours | null;\n  channel_allowlist: string[];     // per-plan (\u2286 auto-publish allowlist MINUS X in Fatia 1 \u2014 OTD-CAD-011)\n  budget_cap_mco: number;          // NOT NULL, in mcoCoins (never USD); cadence requires > 0 (FR-CAD-011)\n  overlap_policy?: \"skip\";\n  catchup_window?: string;         // interval string, default '6 hours'\n  jitter_seconds?: number;\n  program?: CadenceStep[];\n  projectedCostMco?: number;       // client per-cycle projection echoed back by the fn (FR-CAD-011)\n  name?: string;\n  enabled?: boolean;               // arming activates the plan (is_active); default true\n}\n\nexport interface CadencePlanResponse {\n  plan_id: string;\n  next_run_at: string;             // ISO UTC\n  projected_cost_mco: number;\n}\n\n/** Arm (or re-arm) a cadence plan via the `cadence-plan` edge fn. 0 mco \u2014 never debits the balance. */\nexport function useCadencePlan() {\n  return useMutation<CadencePlanResponse, Error, CadencePlanRequest>({\n    mutationFn: async (body): Promise<CadencePlanResponse> => {\n      const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke(\"cadence-plan\", { body });\n      if (error) throw new Error(await edgeErrorMessage(error, \"Falha ao armar a cad\u00eancia.\"));\n      const res = data as (CadencePlanResponse & { error?: string }) | null;\n      if (!res?.plan_id) throw new Error(res?.error ?? \"A cad\u00eancia n\u00e3o retornou um plano v\u00e1lido.\");\n      return res;\n    },\n  });\n}\n\nexport interface CadenceDispatchRow {\n  id: string;\n  channel: string;\n  status: string;\n  occurrence_at: string;\n  created_at: string;\n  error: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface CadenceStatus {\n  next_run_at: string | null;\n  dispatches: CadenceDispatchRow[];\n}\n\n/**\n * Poll a plan's arm state (`next_run_at`) + recent dispatches. Enabled while `planId` is set; fixed 20s\n * interval (cadence is recurrent \u2014 there is no terminal state to stop on). Mold: useVeoPoll.\n */\nexport function useCadenceRunPoll(planId: string | undefined, limit = 8) {\n  return useQuery<CadenceStatus>({\n    queryKey: [\"cadence-dispatches\", planId],\n    enabled: Boolean(planId),\n    refetchInterval: () => (planId ? 20_000 : false),\n    queryFn: async (): Promise<CadenceStatus> => {\n      // next_run_at from autopilot_plans (owner-scoped RLS; the legacy column is typed).\n      const { data: plan } = await supabase\n        .from(\"autopilot_plans\")\n        .select(\"next_run_at\")\n        .eq(\"id\", planId!)\n        .maybeSingle();\n      const next_run_at = (plan as { next_run_at?: string | null } | null)?.next_run_at ?? null;\n\n      // Recent dispatches (SELECT-own RLS). The cadence_dispatches table may not yet be in the generated\n      // Supabase types for this environment (migration 20260716120000 pending regen), so this leg is\n      // fail-soft: on any error it yields [] and the inspector still shows next_run_at.\n      let dispatches: CadenceDispatchRow[] = [];\n      try {\n        // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any\n        const client = supabase as any;\n        const { data, error } = await client\n          .from(\"cadence_dispatches\")\n          .select(\"id, channel, status, occurrence_at, created_at, error\")\n          .eq(\"plan_id\", planId!)\n          .order(\"created_at\", { ascending: false })\n          .limit(limit);\n        if (!error && Array.isArray(data)) dispatches = data as CadenceDispatchRow[];\n      } catch { /* fail-soft \u2014 ledger not yet available in this environment */ }\n\n      return { next_run_at, dispatches };\n    },\n  });\n}\n"
}
```

## 🤖 Claude *(18:24:22)*

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

Now I'm building out the CadenceInspector component, drawing from the patterns I've established with PublishSocialInspector for asset selection and CinematicVideoInspector for the recurrence logic. I need a subject picker for creative assets scoped to the owner, a recurrence editor with frequency toggles and day selectors that adapt based on whether it's daily, weekly, or monthly, time inputs with five-minute increments, and optional quiet hours configuration.

I'm adding a channel allowlist multi-select pulling from the available destinations but excluding X, a budget cap input with a minimum of 12, and a projected cost calculator that sums the selected channels' costs and determines how many cycles fit within the budget cap. The arm button triggers the useCadencePlan mutation to store the plan ID, and polling via useCadenceRunPoll displays the next scheduled run and recent dispatch history.

I'm setting up the channel options and cost mapping, then carefully styling the gold display using the `mc-gold` class with the Coins icon, calculating the projected cost per cycle and occurrences until the budget cap is reached.

Now I'm building the display to show the cost per cycle in gold, and if there's a cost, the projected total with cycle count; otherwise, a message explaining that this tier offers free publishing on selected channels with the budget cap protecting against future paid channels. For the arm action, I'm constructing the CadencePlanRequest from the node data, setting status to running on invoke, then updating with the plan ID and output data on success or marking as failed on error. The arm button is only enabled when there's a source asset, at least one channel selected, a valid budget cap, and proper recurrence configuration.

Writing the inspector component...

</details>

```json
  "content": "import { useMemo, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { CalendarClock, Loader2, Repeat, Coins, ImageIcon, Film, Check, Clock, MoonStar } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { toast } from \"sonner\";\nimport { Button } from \"@/components/ui/button\";\nimport { Input } from \"@/components/ui/input\";\nimport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\";\nimport { useCanvasStore } from \"@/store/useCanvasStore\";\nimport { supabase } from \"@/integrations/supabase/client\";\nimport { useCreativeAssets } from \"@/hooks/useCreativeAssets\";\nimport { useCadencePlan, useCadenceRunPoll } from \"@/hooks/useCadencePlan\";\nimport { StorageImg } from \"@/components/StorageMedia\";\nimport { type CadenceData, type CadenceFrequency } from \"@/types/canvas\";\nimport { Section, InspectorHeader } from \"../shared\";\n\ninterface Props {\n  node: { id: string; data: CadenceData };\n  projectId: string;\n}\n\nconst FREQUENCIES: { id: CadenceFrequency; label: string }[] = [\n  { id: \"daily\", label: \"Di\u00e1ria\" },\n  { id: \"weekly\", label: \"Semanal\" },\n  { id: \"monthly\", label: \"Mensal\" },\n];\n\n// ISO weekday 1=Mon \u2026 7=Sun.\nconst WEEKDAYS = [\n  { id: 1, label: \"Seg\" },\n  { id: 2, label: \"Ter\" },\n  { id: 3, label: \"Qua\" },\n  { id: 4, label: \"Qui\" },\n  { id: 5, label: \"Sex\" },\n  { id: 6, label: \"S\u00e1b\" },\n  { id: 7, label: \"Dom\" },\n];\n\n// Fatia-1 channel allowlist \u2014 the distinct channels of PUBLISH_DESTINATIONS MINUS X/Twitter (OTD-CAD-011).\n// The `id` is the channel value sent to cadence-plan (matches channel_profiles SPEC).\nconst CADENCE_CHANNELS: { id: string; label: string }[] = [\n  { id: \"instagram\", label: \"Instagram\" },\n  { id: \"tiktok\", label: \"TikTok\" },\n  { id: \"youtube\", label: \"YouTube\" },\n  { id: \"facebook\", label: \"Facebook\" },\n  { id: \"linkedin\", label: \"LinkedIn\" },\n  { id: \"pinterest\", label: \"Pinterest\" },\n];\n\n// TODO(FR-CAD-011 / OTD-CAD-011): the per-channel mco SSOT is NOT declared in the sealed spaces-cadence BoK\n// (05-sdd / 06-data-model leave channel_allowlist per-plan without a cost table; the cadence-plan edge fn\n// echoes this client projection back with the SAME TODO). Fatia-1 publish sinks are free (0 mco, mirroring\n// publishSocial estimateNodeCost=0); X-post-with-link (45 mco \u2014 the FRD FR-CAD-011 example) stays OUT of the\n// Fatia-1 allowlist. Replace with a server-side table once one is declared.\nconst CADENCE_CHANNEL_COST_MCO: Record<string, number> = {\n  instagram: 0,\n  tiktok: 0,\n  youtube: 0,\n  facebook: 0,\n  linkedin: 0,\n  pinterest: 0,\n};\n\nconst MINUTE_STEPS = [0, 5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30, 35, 40, 45, 50, 55];\n// The live DB floor on autopilot_plans.budget_cap_mco (viral-autopilot base migration) \u2014 the edge maps a\n// value below it to a clean 422; we surface the floor here so the operator never trips it blindly.\nconst BUDGET_FLOOR_MCO = 12;\n\nexport function CadenceInspector({ node }: Props) {\n  const updateNodeData = useCanvasStore((s) => s.updateNodeData);\n  const { data: assets, isLoading } = useCreativeAssets();\n  const arm = useCadencePlan();\n  const poll = useCadenceRunPoll(node.data.planId);\n\n  const d = node.data;\n  const rec = d.recurrence;\n\n  const set = (patch: Partial<CadenceData>) => updateNodeData(node.id, { ...patch, status: \"idle\", error: undefined });\n  const setRec = (patch: Partial<CadenceData[\"recurrence\"]>) => set({ recurrence: { ...rec, ...patch } });\n\n  const publishable = (assets ?? []).filter((a) => a.kind === \"image\" || a.kind === \"video\");\n\n  const toggleChannel = (id: string) => {\n    const next = d.channelAllowlist.includes(id)\n      ? d.channelAllowlist.filter((c) => c !== id)\n      : [...d.channelAllowlist, id];\n    set({ channelAllowlist: next });\n  };\n  const toggleDay = (day: number) => {\n    const next = rec.days.includes(day) ? rec.days.filter((x) => x !== day) : [...rec.days, day].sort((a, b) => a - b);\n    setRec({ days: next });\n  };\n\n  // \u2500\u2500 Projected per-cycle cost (FR-CAD-011): \u03a3 (mco of each selected channel) \u00d7 occurrences until the cap. \u2500\u2500\n  const projection = useMemo(() => {\n    const perCycle = d.channelAllowlist.reduce((sum, c) => sum + (CADENCE_CHANNEL_COST_MCO[c] ?? 0), 0);\n    const cap = Number.isFinite(d.budgetCapMco) ? d.budgetCapMco : 0;\n    const occurrences = perCycle > 0 ? Math.floor(cap / perCycle) : null;\n    const projectedTotal = perCycle > 0 && occurrences != null ? perCycle * occurrences : 0;\n    return { perCycle, occurrences, projectedTotal };\n  }, [d.channelAllowlist, d.budgetCapMco]);\n\n  const recurrenceValid =\n    rec.frequency === \"daily\" || (Array.isArray(rec.days) && rec.days.length > 0);\n  const canArm =\n    Boolean(d.sourceAssetId) &&\n    d.channelAllowlist.length > 0 &&\n    d.budgetCapMco > 0 &&\n    recurrenceValid &&\n    !arm.isPending;\n\n  const armed = d.status === \"success\" && Boolean(d.planId);\n  const nextRunAt = poll.data?.next_run_at ?? d.output?.nextRunAt ?? null;\n\n  const handleArm = async () => {\n    if (!canArm) {\n      if (!d.sourceAssetId) toast.error(\"Escolha um criativo de origem.\");\n      else if (d.channelAllowlist.length === 0) toast.error(\"Selecione ao menos um canal.\");\n      else if (!recurrenceValid) toast.error(\"Selecione os dias da recorr\u00eancia.\");\n      else if (d.budgetCapMco <= 0) toast.error(\"Defina o teto de or\u00e7amento em mcoCoins.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    updateNodeData(node.id, { status: \"running\", error: undefined });\n    try {\n      const res = await arm.mutateAsync({\n        planId: d.planId,\n        sourceAssetId: d.sourceAssetId!,\n        recurrence: rec,\n        quiet_hours: d.quietHours ?? null,\n        channel_allowlist: d.channelAllowlist,\n        budget_cap_mco: d.budgetCapMco,\n        overlap_policy: d.overlapPolicy ?? \"skip\",\n        catchup_window: d.catchupWindow ?? \"6 hours\",\n        jitter_seconds: d.jitterSeconds ?? 0,\n        program: d.program,\n        projectedCostMco: projection.projectedTotal,\n        enabled: d.enabled,\n      });\n      updateNodeData(node.id, {\n        status: \"success\",\n        planId: res.plan_id,\n        output: { nextRunAt: res.next_run_at, projectedCostMco: res.projected_cost_mco, armedAt: new Date().toISOString() },\n        error: undefined,\n      });\n      toast.success(d.planId ? \"Cad\u00eancia reagendada \u2713\" : \"Cad\u00eancia armada \u2014 roda sozinha a partir de agora \u2713\");\n    } catch (e) {\n      const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);\n      updateNodeData(node.id, { status: \"failed\", error: msg });\n      toast.error(msg);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const quietOn = Boolean(d.quietHours);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex h-full flex-col\">\n      <div className=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto\">\n        <InspectorHeader icon={<CalendarClock className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5\" />} label={d.label} id={node.id} />\n\n        {/* \u2500\u2500 Subject \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500 */}\n        <Section title=\"Criativo de origem\">\n          {isLoading ? (\n            <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground\">\n              <Loader2 className=\"h-3 w-3 animate-spin\" /> carregando seus criativos\u2026\n            </div>\n          ) : publishable.length === 0 ? (\n            <p className=\"text-[11px] text-muted-foreground\">\n              Nenhum criativo ainda \u2014 gere uma imagem ou v\u00eddeo no Spaces primeiro.\n            </p>\n          ) : (\n            <div className=\"grid max-h-40 grid-cols-3 gap-1.5 overflow-y-auto\">\n              {publishable.slice(0, 30).map((a) => {\n                const selected = d.sourceAssetId === a.id;\n                const src = supabase.storage.from(a.storage_bucket).getPublicUrl(a.storage_key).data.publicUrl;\n                return (\n                  <button\n                    key={a.id}\n                    type=\"button\"\n                    onClick={() => set({ sourceAssetId: a.id, sourceAssetUrl: src, sourceAssetKind: a.kind === \"video\" ? \"video\" : \"image\" })}\n                    className={cn(\n                      \"group relative aspect-square overflow-hidden rounded-md border transition-all\",\n                      selected ? \"border-accent ring-2 ring-accent/40\" : \"border-border hover:border-accent/50\",\n                    )}\n                    title={a.title ?? a.kind}\n                  >\n                    <StorageImg src={src} alt={a.title ?? a.kind} className=\"h-full w-full object-cover\" />\n                    <span className=\"absolute left-1 top-1 rounded bg-black/60 p-0.5 text-white\">\n                      {a.kind === \"video\" ? <Film className=\"h-2.5 w-2.5\" /> : <ImageIcon className=\"h-2.5 w-2.5\" />}\n                    </span>\n                    {selected && (\n                      <span className=\"absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-accent/20\">\n                        <Check className=\"h-5 w-5 text-accent\" />\n                      </span>\n                    )}\n                  </button>\n                );\n              })}\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </Section>\n\n        {/* \u2500\u2500 Recurrence \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500 */}\n        <Section title=\"Recorr\u00eancia\">\n          <div className=\"grid grid-cols-3 gap-2\">\n            {FREQUENCIES.map((f) => (\n              <button\n                key={f.id}\n                type=\"button\"\n                onClick={() => setRec({ frequency: f.id, days: [] })}\n                className={cn(\n                  \"h-9 rounded-md border text-xs font-medium transition-colors\",\n                  rec.frequency === f.id\n                    ? \"border-accent bg-accent/5 text-foreground ring-1 ring-accent/30\"\n                    : \"border-border bg-muted/40 text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground\",\n                )}\n              >\n                {f.label}\n              </button>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n\n          {rec.frequency === \"weekly\" && (\n            <div className=\"mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5\">\n              {WEEKDAYS.map((w) => (\n                <button\n                  key={w.id}\n                  type=\"button\"\n                  onClick={() => toggleDay(w.id)}\n                  className={cn(\n                    \"h-8 w-10 rounded-md border text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors\",\n                    rec.days.includes(w.id)\n                      ? \"border-accent bg-accent/5 text-foreground ring-1 ring-accent/30\"\n                      : \"border-border bg-muted/40 text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted\",\n                  )}\n                >\n                  {w.label}\n                </button>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          )}\n\n          {rec.frequency === \"monthly\" && (\n            <div className=\"mt-2 grid grid-cols-7 gap-1\">\n              {Array.from({ length: 31 }, (_, i) => i + 1).map((day) => (\n                <button\n                  key={day}\n                  type=\"button\"\n                  onClick={() => toggleDay(day)}\n                  className={cn(\n                    \"h-7 rounded border text-[10px] font-medium transition-colors\",\n                    rec.days.includes(day)\n                      ? \"border-accent bg-accent/5 text-foreground ring-1 ring-accent/30\"\n                      : \"border-border bg-muted/40 text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted\",\n                  )}\n                >\n                  {day}\n                </button>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          )}\n\n          <div className=\"mt-3 flex items-center gap-2\">\n            <Clock className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground\" />\n            <select\n              value={rec.hours}\n              onChange={(e) => setRec({ hours: Number(e.target.value) })}\n              className=\"h-8 flex-1 rounded-md border border-border bg-muted/40 px-2 text-xs\"\n            >\n              {Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, h) => h).map((h) => (\n                <option key={h} value={h}>{String(h).padStart(2, \"0\")}h</option>\n              ))}\n            </select>\n            <span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">:</span>\n            <select\n              value={rec.minutes}\n              onChange={(e) => setRec({ minutes: Number(e.target.value) })}\n              className=\"h-8 flex-1 rounded-md border border-border bg-muted/40 px-2 text-xs\"\n            >\n              {MINUTE_STEPS.map((m) => (\n                <option key={m} value={m}>{String(m).padStart(2, \"0\")}</option>\n              ))}\n            </select>\n          </div>\n          <p className=\"mt-1.5 flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground\">\n            <Repeat className=\"h-3 w-3\" /> Fuso: {rec.tz} \u00b7 convertido para UTC no servidor\n          </p>\n        </Section>\n\n        {/* \u2500\u2500 Quiet hours (optional) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500 */}\n        <Section\n          title=\"Hor\u00e1rio de sil\u00eancio\"\n          action={\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              onClick={() => set({ quietHours: quietOn ? null : { start: \"22:00\", end: \"07:00\", days: [] } })}\n              className={cn(\n                \"inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium transition-colors\",\n                quietOn ? \"border-accent bg-accent/5 text-foreground\" : \"border-border bg-muted/40 text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted\",\n              )}\n            >\n              <MoonStar className=\"h-3 w-3\" /> {quietOn ? \"ativo\" : \"desligado\"}\n            </button>\n          }\n        >\n          {quietOn && d.quietHours ? (\n            <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n              <Input\n                type=\"time\"\n                value={d.quietHours.start}\n                onChange={(e) => set({ quietHours: { ...d.quietHours!, start: e.target.value } })}\n                className=\"h-8 text-xs\"\n              />\n              <span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">at\u00e9</span>\n              <Input\n                type=\"time\"\n                value={d.quietHours.end}\n                onChange={(e) => set({ quietHours: { ...d.quietHours!, end: e.target.value } })}\n                className=\"h-8 text-xs\"\n              />\n            </div>\n          ) : (\n            <p className=\"text-[11px] text-muted-foreground\">Nenhuma janela de sil\u00eancio \u2014 publica em qualquer hor\u00e1rio da recorr\u00eancia.</p>\n          )}\n        </Section>\n\n        {/* \u2500\u2500 Channels \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500 */}\n        <Section title=\"Canais\">\n          <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-2\">\n            {CADENCE_CHANNELS.map((c) => {\n              const on = d.channelAllowlist.includes(c.id);\n              return (\n                <button\n                  key={c.id}\n                  type=\"button\"\n                  onClick={() => toggleChannel(c.id)}\n                  className={cn(\n                    \"h-9 rounded-md border text-xs font-medium transition-colors\",\n                    on\n                      ? \"border-accent bg-accent/5 text-foreground ring-1 ring-accent/30\"\n                      : \"border-border bg-muted/40 text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground\",\n                  )}\n                >\n                  {c.label}\n                </button>\n              );\n            })}\n          </div>\n          <p className=\"mt-1.5 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground\">X/Twitter fica fora desta cad\u00eancia (custo por post \u2014 OTD-CAD-011).</p>\n        </Section>\n\n        {/* \u2500\u2500 Budget cap \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500 */}\n        <Section title=\"Teto de or\u00e7amento (mcoCoins)\">\n          <Input\n            type=\"number\"\n            min={BUDGET_FLOOR_MCO}\n            step={1}\n            value={d.budgetCapMco}\n            onChange={(e) => set({ budgetCapMco: Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(e.target.value) || 0)) })}\n            className=\"text-sm\"\n          />\n          <p className=\"mt-1 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground\">\n            M\u00ednimo {BUDGET_FLOOR_MCO} mco. Limita o gasto total da cad\u00eancia (nunca em USD).\n          </p>\n        </Section>\n\n        {/* \u2500\u2500 Projected per-cycle cost (FR-CAD-011) \u2014 em GOLD (MIV) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500 */}\n        <Section title=\"Custo projetado\">\n          <div className=\"rounded-md border border-[color:var(--gold-dim)] bg-[color:var(--gold-veil)] p-2.5\">\n            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n              <span className=\"text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground\">Por ciclo</span>\n              <span className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-mono text-lg font-semibold text-glow-gold\">\n                {projection.perCycle} <Coins className=\"h-4 w-4 mc-gold\" />\n              </span>\n            </div>\n            {projection.perCycle > 0 && projection.occurrences != null ? (\n              <div className=\"mt-1 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px]\">\n                <span className=\"text-muted-foreground\">Proje\u00e7\u00e3o at\u00e9 o teto</span>\n                <span className=\"mc-gold font-mono\">\n                  {projection.projectedTotal} mco \u00b7 {projection.occurrences} ciclos\n                </span>\n              </div>\n            ) : (\n              <p className=\"mt-1 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground\">\n                Gr\u00e1tis nesta fatia \u2014 os canais selecionados n\u00e3o consomem mcoCoins. O teto de {d.budgetCapMco} mco\n                protege futuros canais pagos.\n              </p>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        </Section>\n\n        {/* \u2500\u2500 Status / poll \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500 */}\n        {armed && (\n          <Section title=\"Estado da cad\u00eancia\">\n            <div className=\"rounded-md border border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/5 p-2.5 text-[11px] text-emerald-300\">\n              {nextRunAt\n                ? `Pr\u00f3xima execu\u00e7\u00e3o: ${new Date(nextRunAt).toLocaleString(\"pt-BR\")}`\n                : \"Plano armado \u2014 aguardando o pr\u00f3ximo tick.\"}\n            </div>\n            {(poll.data?.dispatches?.length ?? 0) > 0 && (\n              <ul className=\"mt-2 space-y-1\">\n                {poll.data!.dispatches.map((row) => (\n                  <li key={row.id} className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded border border-border bg-muted/30 px-2 py-1 text-[10px]\">\n                    <span className=\"truncate text-foreground\">{row.channel}</span>\n                    <span className=\"font-mono text-muted-foreground\">{row.status}</span>\n                    <span className=\"shrink-0 text-muted-foreground\">\n                      {new Date(row.occurrence_at).toLocaleString(\"pt-BR\", { day: \"2-digit\", month: \"2-digit\", hour: \"2-digit\", minute: \"2-digit\" })}\n                    </span>\n                  </li>\n                ))}\n              </ul>\n            )}\n          </Section>\n        )}\n\n        {d.error && (\n          <Section>\n            <div className=\"rounded-md border border-destructive/30 bg-destructive/10 p-2.5 text-[11px] text-destructive\">\n              {d.error}\n            </div>\n          </Section>\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"border-t border-border bg-card p-3\">\n        <Button\n          type=\"button\"\n          variant=\"accent\"\n          className=\"w-full gap-2\"\n          disabled={!canArm}\n          onClick={handleArm}\n          title={!d.sourceAssetId ? \"Escolha um criativo\" : undefined}\n        >\n          {arm.isPending ? <Loader2 className=\"h-4 w-4 animate-spin\" /> : <CalendarClock className=\"h-4 w-4\" />}\n          {arm.isPending ? \"Armando\u2026\" : armed ? \"Reagendar cad\u00eancia\" : \"Armar cad\u00eancia\"}\n        </Button>\n        <p className=\"mt-1.5 text-center text-[9px] text-muted-foreground\">\n          Roda no tick recorrente \u00b7 gr\u00e1tis para armar \u00b7 publica na sua fila owner-scoped\n        </p>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```
